#### Gabby

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=4866  
Words: 457

[image: Gabby]
Looks like: Schnauzer, Poodle mix
Female, 13 years old (estimated), 37 pounds
Foster needed
Gorgeous Gabby may be enjoying her golden years, but this sweet senior Schnoodle still has plenty of pep in her step! She loves her daily walks, basking in the sunshine, and staying close to her people for gentle pets and affection. With her moderate energy level, Gabby is the perfect companion for someone who enjoys a leisurely stroll followed by a cozy cuddle session on the couch.
Gabby is as easygoing as they come. She gets along wonderfully with both dogs and cats, making her a great fit for many types of homes. She isn't demanding or overbearing - just a calm, loving presence with soulful eyes that have a way of capturing everyone's heart. Gabby is also delightfully quiet, usually only offering a bark or two every few days when she's hoping for a little extra attention.
One of Gabby's many wonderful qualities is that she is fully housetrained, according to her foster. In fact, she arrived without even a single accident, which is a rare and impressive testament to what a good girl she is.
Since arriving, Gabby has been receiving the medical care she needs to help her feel her best. She is currently being treated for Cushing's disease and is working toward losing approximately 9 pounds. Her weight loss journey will take time, even with reduced calories, and may require several months of patience and consistency. Reaching a healthier weight is an important step before she can safely undergo a dental cleaning and have a couple of lipomas removed.
Gabby is also an enthusiastic eater and has a habit of consuming things she shouldn't. Her future family will need to provide a safe environment by keeping potentially harmful items out of reach, including products such as snail or rat bait.
Gabby is a special senior girl who deserves a home where her health needs are understood and where she can spend her days feeling safe, loved, and cherished. If you believe life's sweetest companions only get better with age, Gabby is ready to show you just how wonderful a senior dog can be.
Gabby joined the POMDR pack after her loving guardian was no longer able to care for her.

#### Brisket

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=4892  
Words: 393

[image: Brisket]
Looks like: Shepherd, Chow Chow mix
Male, 8 years old (estimated), 46 pounds
Recently Adopted
This handsome, easygoing guy is looking for a family to call his own. With his big smile, gentle nature, and loyal heart, Brisket is the kind of dog who quickly becomes your favorite sidekick. He loves leisurely walks where he can stop to sniff all the interesting smells, then he's perfectly happy relaxing at home right by your side. Whether you're running errands, visiting family, or enjoying a pet-friendly patio, Brisket is happy just being part of the adventure.
Brisket is a social sweetheart who has impressed everyone he's met. He greets new people with a calm, friendly attitude, has done well around children, and gets along beautifully with dogs of all sizes. He's also been an absolute gentleman with cats, happily coexisting without causing any drama. His gentle personality shines through in everything he does and if you catch him proudly carrying around a toy you'll be treated to one of his many adorable quirks!
At home, Brisket is a mellow companion who enjoys following his favorite person from room to room, always wanting to be close. Like many deeply devoted dogs, he forms a strong bond with his people and loves cuddles, pets, and kisses. He truly thrives on companionship and is happiest when he can simply hang out wherever you are, quietly relaxing nearby until it's time for another walk or a little attention.
Brisket's ideal home is one with patient, loving adopters who understand that he's still working through some separation anxiety. A work-from-home household or one where someone is home often would be a wonderful fit as he continues building confidence when left alone. In return, you'll gain an incredibly loyal, gentle best friend who will greet you like you're the best part of every day. If you're looking for a sweet, affectionate companion to share life's simple joys, Brisket can't wait to meet you!
Brisket came to us from Hitchcock Road Animal Services after they reached out to see if we could help him get back on his feet. He had been found in pretty rough shape, and with lots of care, patience, and love, he's been given the chance to heal and start fresh.
Sponsored By: Brody; Tetyana Margolina in memory of Marty, Sherwood and Leonid

#### Otter

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=4938  
Words: 512

[image: Otter]
Looks like: Chihuahua Mix
Male, 12 years old (estimated), 14 pounds
Meet Otter, a sweet little gentleman with a big heart and a serious appreciation for naps, snacks, and being close to his favorite person! Otter is a low-energy, affectionate guy who spends about 80% of his day happily snoozing. He's house-trained, rarely barks, and communicates mostly through adorable little whines when he wants attention or isn't feeling his best. He loves following his person around and is happiest when he can be picked up and tucked beside you on the sofa. Once you stop snuggling him, don't be surprised if he gently puts his head on your chest to request more!
Otter is a bit shy and has weak rear legs, so he isn't a candidate for long walks or a high-energy lifestyle. He enjoys leisurely strolls, happily says hello to dogs and people he meets, and then is perfectly content to continue on his way. Because his back legs tire easily, he may drag his rear paws and scrape them after walking for a while, and he needs to be carried up and down steps. He has a wonderfully laid-back attitude toward the world and is also quite comfortable in the car, where he'll simply sit or lie down for the ride.
This little guy has plenty of personality, too! He loves treats so much that they can even be added to his meals, and sometimes he enjoys being spoon-fed. He doesn't have much interest in traditional toys, but he does occasionally toss his doggy bed around and burrow underneath it. He gets along well with other animals, showing some polite curiosity before deciding they're really not all that interesting. He's also happy to meet new people and should do well with respectful children.
Otter's ideal home is a quiet one with someone who has plenty of time and affection to give. He doesn't need a busy schedule or lots of exercise - he'd much rather spend the day beside his person, take a gentle midday walk, enjoy some treats, and curl up on the couch. He does get excited when his person returns home and may howl until he settles down, but once he's calm, he's back to his favorite activities: following you, snuggling, and napping.
Otter is a special little guy looking for a special person who understands that his slower pace and weak back legs are simply part of who he is. In return, he'll give you endless companionship, sweet kisses, hilarious quirks, and a whole lot of love. If your idea of the perfect day involves a cozy couch, a devoted little shadow, plenty of affection, and maybe a few treats from the refrigerator, Otter may be your perfect match!
Otter comes to us from the Monterey SPCA after his guardian passed away.

Axel joined the POMDR pack from Hitchcock Road Animal Shelter after he was picked up as a stray.

#### Fifa

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=4950  
Words: 391

[image: Fifa]
Looks like: Schnauzer mix
Female, 10 years old (estimated), 15 pounds
This sweet tripod girl is proving every day that three legs are more than enough to live a happy, love-filled life. She gave birth to 6 puppies six weeks ago and is looking forward to finding her forever home now that the puppies are just about weaned.
Fifa has never met a stranger or a dog she doesn't like! She greets new people with happy tail wags, gentle hand kisses, and her soulful eyes, and she's eager to make friends with every pup she meets by exchanging sniffs and kisses. She's house-trained, rides calmly in the car, sleeps through the night, and has wonderful manners around other dogs. She's even been a respectful, curious neighbor to her foster's bunny, proving just how gentle her heart really is.
One of the best things about Fifa is how affectionate she is. She loves belly rubs, treats, cuddles, and being close to her people. Her adorable little morning grunts when she wakes up are guaranteed to make you smile, and while she's mostly the perfect lady, she has just enough mischievous charm to keep life fun; like the time she quietly helped herself to half a sandwich from the coffee table! She's also incredibly patient and trusting, even during her recovery, making grooming and care a breeze.
Fifa would thrive in almost any loving home, especially one with another friendly dog and people who enjoy having a loyal companion by their side. She does have Valley Fever which is a fungal infection. She needs to continue her Valley Fever treatment once the puppies are weaned.
She embraces each day with resilience, sweetness, and an endless supply of love to give. If you're looking for a gentle best friend who will fill your days with snuggles, kisses, and unconditional love, Fifa is ready to steal your heart.
Fifa joined the POMDR family after the staff at the Los Banos Animal Shelter recognized her exceptional temperament and health challenges and reached out to find Her a rescue placement.

#### Praline

Words: 381

[image: Praline]
Looks like: Poodle mix
Female, 13 years old (estimated), 9 pounds
If you are looking for a sweet treat without the extra sugar and calories, then this Praline is made for you! Praline is a mellow and gentle soul who is looking for lots of affection, yummy treats, and a calm, peaceful home where she can enjoy a slower pace of life. Get comfortable on the sofa with a good book in your hand, and this little delight will be happy to curl up next to you. When it is feeding time, she will perk right up and perform a little twirly dance on her back feet. For bedtime, she loves to be under the blankets snuggled up by your side. She is happiest when she can be near the people she loves all day, every day. Before joining our program, she did not get a chance to live life to the fullest, and she is eager to catch up on the plush, pampered poodle lifestyle.
At her foster home, Praline gets along well with her canine siblings, even sharing a bed with dogs much larger than her! She prefers gentle, calm dogs that respect her personal space, speed, and size. She is a perfect car-ride companion, either looking out the window or snoozing in her seat. At home, she is fully house-trained and low maintenance. She would do best in a house without stairs, or where she can be carried up and down stairs, due to some mobility and vision limitations. This little survivor had a complete mastectomy and is currently on Meloxicam.
Praline joined us from Santa Cruz County Animal Services after she was found as a stray. Due to her senior status and her need for medical attention, they reached out to find her the perfect retirement home. Praline's ideal day is sitting by the open door, snuggled by your side as you enjoy a good book. If you're looking for a gentle soul to share your days with, Praline may be the perfect match.

#### Simba

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=5012  
Words: 301

[image: Simba]
Looks like: Terrier mix
Male, 10 years old (estimated), 8 pounds
Simba is the kind of gentleman who makes an impression without ever asking for much. This sweet senior boy is polite, easygoing, good-natured, and wonderfully independent. He's happy to spend his days at a leisurely pace, enjoying the company of his people and taking in the world around him. Simba is also a polite pup with other dogs and does well sharing his space with canine friends. He has a calm, unassuming personality and seems perfectly content to simply be part of the family.
Simba was reportedly wandering around town as a stray for several weeks before finally making his way to the shelter. Considering this gentleman is fairly slow-moving and very much enjoys life at his own pace, we're not quite sure how he managed to become such a seasoned neighborhood explorer! What we do know is that Simba is a really lovely boy who deserves a comfortable place to call home. If you're looking for a sweet, polite, low-key companion who doesn't need much fanfare to be happy, Simba might be your guy. He's ready to leave his wandering days behind and settle into a forever home where he can finally put his paws up and enjoy the good life.
Little Simba joined us from Hitchcock Road Animal Services after he was found as a stray. Due to the shelter's full status and his senior age, they felt he would do best to join a senior focused rescue to land in his loving forever home.

#### Ashanti

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=5029  
Words: 343

[image: Ashanti]
Looks like: German Shepherd, Lab mix
Female, 9 years old (estimated), 55 pounds
Ashanti came to a local shelter after her elderly guardian suffered a fall and was unable to move for several days. When help finally arrived, they found Ashanti outside, without the care or resources she desperately needed. She was emaciated beyond belief, with painfully overgrown nails curling beneath her feet, swollen legs, severely damaged skin, and large areas of missing fur. On top of everything her body had endured, she was understandably frightened by this strange new world around her.
But beneath all of that neglect is a remarkably sweet girl who seems incredibly grateful for every gentle touch, kind word, and bit of care she receives. She is learning that hands can bring comfort, that meals will keep coming, and that she is finally safe. We can't wait to watch Ashanti blossom.
We know there is a beautiful dog waiting to emerge as her body heals and her confidence grows, but getting there will take time, patience, and a very special person. Ashanti is looking for an experienced home familiar with German Shepherds and with the patience to let her set the pace. Her adopter will need to be comfortable with the long game: allowing her to decompress, helping her learn what it means to be cared for, and celebrating the tiny victories along the way. She doesn't need someone looking for an instant best friend. She needs someone willing to earn her trust and walk beside her as she discovers the life she should have had all along.
Ashanti has already survived so much. Now she gets to learn what it feels like to be cherished. We have a feeling that watching this girl come back to life is going to be something pretty special.
[Adopt Ashanti](https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/POMDRAdoptionQuestionnaire.php?dogname=Ashanti)[Sponsor Ashanti](https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/POMDRSponsorDog.php?dogname=Ashanti)
Are you interested in learning more about Ashanti? For more medical and behavior information, press the Adopt Ashanti button and fill out our online adoption questionnaire. Our Adoption Coordinator will give you as much information as we have.

#### Tartar Sauce

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=5031  
Words: 370

[image: Tartar Sauce]
Looks like: Chihuahua mix
Male, 3 months old, 4 pounds
What happens when you mix Mayo and Relish? You get Tartar Sauce! Tartar Sauce and his siblings surprised the POMDR family in July, and he's the last of the bunch waiting for a family!
Tartar Sauce is a sweet, smart pup. He picks up on stuff quickly, so be prepared to stay ahead of this guy as he learns to navigate the world. Much like his siblings, he is extremely loving, but he is the gentle one, preferring to be with calm dogs and minimizing rough playtime. With older dogs, he has been nothing but kind. He would do best in an adult only household. When confused, he gives a cute head-tilt sure to melt your heart.
Tartar Sauce prefers not to be handled too much by new people, but once he gets to know you, he is your faithful companion. He would do best in a quiet and patient household, where he is slowly introduced to the many new sights, sounds, and smells in the world. Like any other puppy, he is in an important developmental period. Adopters should be ready to get him training to help with his socialization and development, especially because he has shown a timid side. He's not a 'take anywhere' type of dog, but if you are looking for a dog to bond with through training, look no further! We are hoping to find Tartar Sauce an adult-only home. Could Tartar Sauce be your secret ingredient?
How did Tartar come to find himself with a senior dog rescue, you may ask? His senior mom was brought into our rescue from a partner shelter. Unknown to everyone, she was heavily pregnant. She gave birth just days after her arrival with us, and Tartar was one of these little ones. We'd love to find him the perfect home that can meet his needs and where he can shine.

Jason is a gentle soul who needs a little extra time and a whole lot of understanding to feel safe in the world. He's a timid boy who is still learning to trust, and he'll need a patient person who is happy to let him move at his own pace. Jason isn't ready to hit the walking trails just yet, and he may never be the adventurous, leash-walking type. But with time, kindness, and a little encouragement, he may surprise us! For now, his happy place is somewhere safe and cozy, with the freedom to take each new step when he's ready.
One of the best things about Jason is how wonderful he is with other dogs! In fact, having a confident canine companion (or even a whole pack!) in his next home is a must for this boy. He takes comfort in his fellow pups and can learn so much by watching them navigate the world. He also does well with kitties, making him a lovely candidate for a home with feline friends, too.
Jason is looking for someone who understands that progress doesn't always happen overnight. He may need extra time to settle in and build trust, but for the right person, helping him discover that the world can be a safe and happy place will be a very special journey.
Jason joins Peace of Mind Dog Rescue from Hitchcock Road Animal Services, where his senior status made him a good fit for our program.

#### Jamie Tart

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=5076  
Words: 364

[image: Jamie Tart]
Looks like: Chihuahua, Pomeranian mixes
Male, 2 months old (estimated), 4 pounds
Adoption pending
Jamie Tart is one of six adorable puppies born to Fifa, who joined Peace of Mind Dog Rescue after being surrendered to a local shelter because her family was unable to support the leg amputation she needed. Shortly after Fifa arrived in our care, we discovered that she had six little surprises on the way!
Now about two months old, Jamie and his siblings are growing like little weeds! Mom, Fifa, weighs approximately 15 pounds, and while we don't know Dad's size or breed, a Wisdom Panel DNA test suggests the puppies are primarily a mix of Chihuahua, Pomeranian, and Poodle.
Jamie Tart is playful, curious, and always ready for his next adventure! He loves chewing on toys, chasing them around, and keeping himself busy. Jamie would thrive in an active household that can keep both his mind and body stimulated with plenty of playtime, enrichment, and opportunities to explore. If you're looking for a fun-loving little companion who will keep you on your toes, Jamie might just be your perfect match!
We anticipate receiving far more adoption questionnaires than we have puppies available. We kindly ask for your patience as we review each application and work to find the best possible match for every puppy and prospective family. We also ask for your understanding if you are not selected, as we simply have only so many puppies to go around!
Adopters will need to commit to a $905 initial investment to adopt a puppy from POMDR: $305 for adoption fee, $300 for refundable neuter deposit, $300 for refundable training deposit. The neuter deposit will be reimbursed once the dog is altered at the POMDR clinic (at no cost to the adopter) or we receive proof of alteration from another clinic. (Adopter must pay for the full alteration if done at an outside clinic.) The training deposit will be refunded once we are presented with proof that the puppy is enrolled in a training class or working with a private trainer.
[Sponsor Jamie Tart](https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/POMDRSponsorDog.php?dogname=Jamie Tart)
Interested in adopting? If you are interested in adopting Jamie Tart, please keep an eye on our website in case the pending adoption doesn't work out and Jamie Tart becomes available again.

(Adopter must pay for the full alter if done at an outside clinic.) The training deposit will be refunded once we are presented with proof that the puppy is enrolled in a training class or working with a private trainer.

#### Diego Martinez

Address: https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/dog.php?id=5080  
Words: 366

[image: Diego Martinez]
Looks like: Chihuahua, Pomeranian
Male, 2 months old (estimated), 4 pounds
Adoption pending
Diego Martinez is one of six adorable puppies born to Fifa, who joined Peace of Mind Dog Rescue after being surrendered to a local shelter because her family was unable to support the leg amputation she needed. Shortly after Fifa arrived in our care, we discovered that she had six little surprises on the way!
Now about two months old, Diego and his siblings are growing like little weeds! Mom, Fifa, weighs approximately 15 pounds, and while we don't know Dad's size or breed, a Wisdom Panel DNA test suggests the puppies are primarily a mix of Chihuahua, Pomeranian, and Poodle.
Diego Martinez is the sleepiest pup of the litter! After a little playtime, he's more than happy to wind down, snuggle up beside you, and enjoy some quality cuddle time. In fact, he'll politely ask to be scooped up and settled into your lap for an epic nap. If your ideal companion is a sweet little sidekick who appreciates cozy afternoons, puppy snuggles, and a good snooze, Diego might just be your perfect match!
We anticipate receiving far more adoption questionnaires than we have puppies available. We kindly ask for your patience as we review each application and work to find the best possible match for every puppy and prospective family. We also ask for your understanding if you are not selected, as we simply have only so many puppies to go around!
Adopters will need to commit to a $905 initial investment to adopt a puppy from POMDR: $305 for adoption fee, $300 for refundable neuter deposit, $300 for refundable training deposit. The neuter deposit will be reimbursed once the dog is altered at the POMDR clinic (at no cost to the adopter) or we receive proof of alteration from another clinic. (Adopter must pay for the full alteration if done at an outside clinic.) The training deposit will be refunded once we are presented with proof that the puppy is enrolled in a training class or working with a private trainer.
[Sponsor Diego Martinez](https://www.peaceofminddogrescue.org/POMDRSponsorDog.php?dogname=Diego Martinez)
Interested in adopting? If you are interested in adopting Diego Martinez, please keep an eye on our website in case the pending adoption doesn't work out and Diego Martinez becomes available again.

Crystal is a sweet, affectionate gal with a gentle and easygoing personality. She enjoys being around her people and is the kind of dog who will happily settle in beside you for companionship and cuddles. Crystal is friendly, loving, and ready to bring warmth and joy to her forever home.
Crystal gets along well with other dogs and would be happy to share her home with a canine companion. Her calm, pleasant nature makes her a wonderful little dog to have around, whether she's relaxing by your side or enjoying time with her furry friends.
